Every response block keeps its raw JSON in crudum with provisor anthropic; every type other than text and tool_use (thinking with its signature, and any type vates does not know) becomes VATES_OPACUM. When the block goes back in the next request (vates_responsum_addere) an Anthropic block is emitted VERBATIM, so signatures and unmodelled fields survive. The Anthropic request builder omits an OPACUM block whose provisor is not anthropic, as the API itself omits another model's thinking.
    symbolum vates_responsum_addere vates_anthropic_corpus
  12. possumnecan I use an API feature or beta header that vates does not model?
    Yes: vates_extra_ponere(p, provisor, clavis, valor) adds a body field (emitted last, after messages) and vates_caput_addere(p, provisor, titulus, valor) adds a header such as anthropic-beta. Both are tagged with a provider name and applied only by that provider's request builder, so code that sets Anthropic extras runs unchanged, without them, under another provider. The fictus provider uses the Anthropic builder, so it does apply extras and headers tagged anthropic.
    symbolum vates_extra_ponere vates_caput_addere

via

include/via.h · lib/via.interrogationes.stml

  1. quomodohow do I join a directory and a file name into one path?
    Put the parts in a chorda array and call via_iungere(partes, numerus, piscina); C89 needs the array filled at run time, not in an initializer. It adds a '/' between two parts only when neither side already has one, skips empty parts, and copies the result into the piscina. It never removes a slash, so "foo/" + "/bar" gives "foo//bar" (run via_normalizare if that matters). Unlike Python's os.path.join an absolute later part does not restart the path: "foo" + "/bar" gives "foo/bar".
    symbolum via_iungere chorda_ex_literis
  2. possumnecan I keep the result of via_nomen or via_directorium after the input path is gone?
    Not always, whatever the header's "every function allocates" says. via_nomen, via_nomen_radix and via_extensio return slices of the input (via_nomen ignores its piscina argument), and via_directorium returns a slice too, except that its answers "." and "/" are fresh copies in the piscina. If the input's bytes are freed or rolled back, the slices go with them. via_iungere and via_normalizare copy into the piscina (except that via_normalizare hands an empty input straight back). Copy with chorda_transcribere when you need a slice to outlive the input.
    symbolum via_nomen via_directorium via_nomen_radix via_extensio via_iungere via_normalizare chorda_transcribere
  3. quomodohow do I turn a relative path into an absolute one?
    via_absoluta joins the current directory (via_directorium_currens, getcwd) with the path and then calls via_normalizare; an input that is already absolute is only normalized. It is purely lexical: symlinks are not resolved and the path does not have to exist. If getcwd fails the input comes back unchanged, still relative and not normalized, with no error signal, so test via_est_absoluta on the result when it matters. Caution (audit 2026-10-18, unfixed): getcwd also "fails" for a valid current directory longer than 4096 bytes (fixed stack buffer, ERANGE, no retry), so via_directorium_currens returns the empty chorda and via_absoluta("sub/../file") returns the relative input unchanged.
    symbolum via_absoluta via_normalizare via_directorium_currens via_est_absoluta
  4. possumnecan via_normalizare handle relative paths that climb more than one level with ../?
    No, not correctly. It resolves '.', '//' and 'x/..' and keeps a leading ".." on a relative path, but a ".." that meets a kept ".." on its stack pops it instead of being kept: "../../foo" comes out as "foo", "../.." as "." and "a/../../.." as ".". A single "../foo" and absolute paths are fine ("/.." is "/"). Do not normalize relative paths that climb more than one level until this is fixed.
    symbolum via_normalizare
  5. quidwhat do via_nomen and via_directorium return for trailing slashes and the root?
    They follow POSIX basename and dirname for trailing slashes: "/foo/bar/" gives "bar" and "/foo". For the root "/", via_nomen returns the empty chorda (POSIX basename returns "/", changed on purpose: the root has no name) and via_directorium returns "/". A name with no slash has directory ".", and so does the empty path. Caution (audit 2026-10-18, unfixed): on an input of 2^31 bytes or more the s32 scan index overflows and no slash is found, so for "/dir/" + 2^31 bytes + "/name.txt" via_nomen returns the whole input, via_directorium returns "." and via_extensio returns "".
    symbolum via_nomen via_directorium
  6. quidwhat counts as the extension of a file name, and what about dotfiles?
    via_extensio returns the last dot and everything after it, dot included ("archive.tar.gz" gives ".gz"). A name starting with a dot has no extension at all, even with a second dot: ".bashrc" and ".config.json" both give "", and via_nomen_radix returns the whole name. Only the last path component is looked at, so a dot in a directory name never counts.
    symbolum via_extensio via_nomen_radix
  7. quomodohow do I check whether a path exists, and should I use via_existit or filum_existit?
    via_existit takes a chorda and uses stat, so it is VERUM for directories and for files you cannot read. filum_existit takes a C string and tries fopen(via, "r"), so it is FALSUM for a file you cannot read (on macOS a readable directory opens, so it is VERUM there too). Both record the answer (X or A) in the fabrica read ledger. via_existit returns FALSUM for an empty path or one of IV * MXXIV bytes or more. Caution (audit 2026-10-18, unfixed): via_existit ignores the chorda length past an embedded NUL byte, because stat sees only the prefix, so "/\0nusquam_zzz" or "/etc/hosts\0/nope" gives VERUM instead of FALSUM.
    symbolum via_existit filum_existit lectiones_notare
  8. curwhy does via_existit write to a ledger file, and what does it cost?
    The build layer (fabrica) records what a generator looked up so it can decide when to rebuild. aedilis's include resolution goes through via_existit, and a header it looked for and did not find is a negative dependency: creating it later would change the result. lectiones_notare does nothing unless the environment variable FABRICA_LECTIONES names a file, so ordinary programs pay one getenv per call on top of the stat.
    symbolum via_existit lectiones_notare · via lib/fabrica.worklog.md

vicus

include/vicus.h · lib/vicus.interrogationes.stml

  1. quomodohow do I build a vicus host with my own app kinds?
    In this order: vicus_creare; vicus_genus_addere for each kind (the byte size of its mount struct, a VicusMontator, a VicusDescriptor and a ctx handed to every mount); vicus_systemata_ponere BEFORE opening; vicus_aperire with the default layout; vicus_stratum_addere only after vicus_aperire (it needs the repository). Then create the dispensator over v->repo with vicus_actiones(v) and vicus_componere (ctx = v), call vicus_dispensatorem_ligare once, and call vicus_pulsare every frame.
    symbolum vicus_creare vicus_genus_addere vicus_systemata_ponere vicus_aperire vicus_stratum_addere vicus_dispensatorem_ligare vicus_pulsare · via lib/vicus_applicatio.c
  2. quidwhat are a tabula, a latus and the acervus in vicus?
    A tabula is a tab (at most VICUS_TABULAE, X, are read) holding two sides: the left latus and a right acervus (stack) whose LAST element is the visible front and which is never empty. Each latus is one app mount; its id, by default "<tab>_<side>_<kind>" (for example "3_sinistrum_scriba") and stored in the layout, is also its branch id, and its branch path "<kind>:<id>" is its scope for actions, figures and Motus.
    symbolum VicusTabula VicusLatus VICUS_TABULAE vicus_latus
  3. quomodohow do I open a pane from inside a command or event handler?
    Call vicus_acervo_aperire(v, genus, argumentum): it only QUEUES a request on the active tab (FALSUM for an unknown kind, no active tab or a NIHIL argument), and the next vicus_pulsare applies it: an existing pane with the same kind and argument comes to the front, else a new one is mounted with the default id, or that id plus _2, _3... if taken; focus goes right. Swapping registries and the Motus gesture in the middle of a dispatch would break the handler that is running. vicus_pulsare ignores a failed request (a failed mount only sets vicus_causa). A new pane is mounted at the size given to vicus_creare, not the current window size. vicus_acervo_mutare instead gives the frontmost pane of that kind in the stack a new argument through facies.argumentum_ponere and brings it to the front without moving focus; only when the stack has no pane of that kind does it mount one (focus then goes right). If that pane has no argumentum_ponere hook, or the hook returns FALSUM, the request fails and nothing new is mounted.
    symbolum vicus_acervo_aperire vicus_acervo_mutare vicus_pulsare
  4. possumnecan I keep a VicusLatus pointer across vicus_pulsare or a focus change?
    No. Panes live by value in the acervus Xar: bringing one to the front copies the VicusLatus structs down one slot, and vicus_acervo_redire truncates the front away, so the same address can hold a different pane afterwards. Look the pane up again with vicus_latus or vicus_latus_focatum. The mount struct (VicusLatus.montatio) never moves.
    symbolum vicus_latus vicus_latus_focatum vicus_acervo_redire vicus_latus_focare
  5. quidwhat is a VicusFacies and what happens to fields my kind does not fill?
    It is what the host takes from a mounted pane: action and figure registries, componere and its ctx, an image source, gesture, pulse, first-click and argument hooks, and flags such as vivit_in_fundo and focus_in_apertura. vicus zeroes the whole struct before calling your VicusDescriptor, so any field you leave out simply means absent (no gesture, no pulse, not living in the background).
    symbolum VicusFacies VicusDescriptor
  6. curwhy does the first click on an unfocused pane only focus it?
    The dispatch strategy sends a press whose component scope is not under Motus.spatium to the host root, and the root handler focuses the side under x (left half or right). Only after focus has moved (Motus already set to the new pane) does it call the pane's optional ictus_primus hook, so a kind such as scriba can choose to act on that first click; without the hook the click only focuses. The hook runs inside the root's action, so it must set repo->scriptor to its own action name for the call, or the pane's owner rules silently refuse its writes.
    symbolum vicus_focum_ponere VicusFacies · via lib/vicus.worklog.md
  7. quomodohow does a background system write a field on the vicus root?
    Register it with vicus_systema_addere(v, titulus, fn, ctx); it is called on every vicus_pulsare and returns VERUM when a frame must be drawn. Inside, write with vicus_radicem_scribere(v, scriptor, titulus, valor), valor NIHIL removing the field. The writer is normally the system's own titulus, declared in the root manifest as an actio with systema="verum" (as horologium and admonitiones are); it must own the field there, otherwise the write is refused and the call returns FALSUM. vicus_systema_addere returns FALSUM for a titulus already registered.
    symbolum vicus_systema_addere vicus_radicem_scribere · via apps/vicus/organum.stml
  8. curwhy does vicus write the pane surface sizes as writer "dispensator"?
    Each app reads superficies_latitudo and superficies_altitudo from its OWN branch, and the apps' owner rules give those fields to "dispensator". In vicus the host plays the dispensator for its mounts: it saves repo->scriptor, writes as "dispensator", and restores. Under any other writer the owner check refuses the write without an error and the branch keeps no surface.
    symbolum vicus_aperire vicus_componere · via lib/vicus.worklog.md
  9. possumnecan I show a clock and battery in the vicus tab bar, and keep tests stable?
    Yes. By default there is no clock (NIHIL), so tests and golden images do not change every minute. App mains call vicus_horologium_ponere(v, vicus_horologium_locale, NIHIL) and vicus_pilam_ponere with a battery port; tests pass a fake clock. Readings are stored as root fields (tempus_*, pila_centesimae, pila_oneratur); the battery is read again only when the clock reading changes (so with no clock it is read only by vicus_pilam_ponere itself, which reads nothing before vicus_aperire has made the repository), and the clock makes vicus_pulsare return VERUM only when the minute or the day changed.
    symbolum vicus_horologium_ponere vicus_horologium_locale vicus_pilam_ponere vicus_pulsare
  10. quomodohow do I find out why a vicus pane did not appear?
    Read vicus_causa(v); it holds only the LAST message. A pane whose kind is not registered (for example a volume saved by a newer host) is kept in the layout but not mounted ("genus ignotum, latus non montatum: ..."), and a mount function that returns FALSUM gives "montatio defecit: <id>". When vicus_aperire itself fails it says "manifestum hospitis malum" or "dispositio tabularum mala". The layout itself lives in volume plagula 'vicus/latera' and is overwritten on each change; it is configuration, not history.
    symbolum vicus_causa vicus_aperire

vicus_applicatio

include/vicus_applicatio.h · lib/vicus_applicatio.interrogationes.stml

  1. quomodohow do I start the vicus host app in a window or a terminal?
    Call thema_initiare, open the volume with vicus_volumen_aperire (-fumus = temporary volume, -volumen PATH, default vicus.volumen), then vicus_applicatio_aedificare(&app, piscina, intern, vol, latitudo, altitudo) with the size in our pixels. Hand the glue app.d, vicus_figurae(app.vicus) and vicus_imago_fons with ctx app.vicus; these never change afterwards. Close the volume with volumen_claudere at exit.
    symbolum vicus_volumen_aperire vicus_applicatio_aedificare vicus_figurae vicus_imago_fons thema_initiare volumen_claudere · via apps/vicus/vicus.c apps/vicus/vicus_terminalis.c
  2. quidwhat does a fresh vicus open with, and which pane kinds can it show?
    Ten tabs: 1 is scriba beside terminale, 2 scriba beside pictor, 3-10 scriba beside scriba, plus the volantes (toast) layer on top. The registered kinds are scriba, pictor, terminale, calendarium, nomenclator, admonitiones, colloquia and volantes (the header's opening comment still lists only the first three). A tab whose kind is unknown or whose mount fails is skipped with its reason on stderr; the rest still run.
    symbolum vicus_applicatio_aedificare vicus_genus_addere vicus_aperire
  3. quomodohow do I open another pane or app from a scriba page in vicus?
    Type a command on the page: $terminale, $scriba(name), $pictor(name), $calendarium, $agenda, $contacts, $notifications or $sessions opens that kind in the active tab's stack on the next pulse; $pictor-next, $pictor-prev and $pictor-new cycle or create pictures. A scriba page name may only use a-z, 0-9, _ and -. $dies or $dies(N) inserts the date N days from today as MM/DD/YYYY.
    symbolum vicus_applicatio_aedificare vicus_acervo_aperire iussum_registrare
  4. quomodohow do I make $question work, and why does it say there is no API port?
    The window app must give a port with vicus_applicatio_quaestionis_portam_ponere(&app, &porta): a VicusPortaQuaestionis with incipere, pulsare and abrumpere. The struct is copied. A NIHIL port, or one missing any of the three functions, clears it, and $question then reports "no API port (window app only)", which is what the terminal twin and the tests see.
    symbolum vicus_applicatio_quaestionis_portam_ponere VicusPortaQuaestionis · via apps/vicus/vicus.c
  5. curwhy does vicus_applicatio not call the model API or parse Markdown itself?
    Plan ruling R9: the PORT plans the answer into logical lines (quaestio_textum_planare). If this file did it, it would include quaestio, vates and http/TLS, and the terminal twin and ludus_tessera's hand-made link list would drag in the whole API stack. The port types live in vicus_quaestio.h so the port can be built without including the whole app.
    symbolum VicusPortaQuaestionis quaestio_textum_planare · via lib/vicus_applicatio.worklog.md include/vicus_quaestio.h
  6. quidwhat happens on the page while $question streams an answer?
    The command is $question<>...</> with free lines below it (otherwise "no free lines below"). The first call only defers ("asking ..."); the second starts the port and returns a job that each pulse lays out with vicus_quaestio_disponere into the FREE lines below the command, never beyond, and the command's head becomes <answered>. When the page is full the port is aborted and the text is cut at the last sentence or line end that fits (else at a word, with "..."); Esc adds [cancelled]; an error after some text adds [error: ...]; an error or an empty answer before any text leaves the page untouched. Caution (audit 2026-10-18, unfixed): the per-pulse layout is not always bounded - an answer line whose indentation plus list/quote marker reaches the pane's inner width makes vicus_quaestio_disponere loop forever, and a hanging-indent answer can lay out to about 4x its length and overflow the responsum_aptare buffer (heap write past the piscina allocation).
    symbolum vicus_quaestio_disponere · via lib/vicus_applicatio.worklog.md
  7. possumnecan I pass any text to vicus_quaestio_disponere?
    Planned text only (what quaestio_textum_planare gives): logical lines, a leading tab marks verbatim code that is hard-broken but never reflowed, and a list or quote marker after the indentation gets a hanging indent. It returns a newline, then <answer> ... </> wrapped to width w with two columns of margin on each side (none below 24 columns), cut to fit l lines. Keep each line's indentation plus marker narrower than the inner width (w minus the margins): at or above it the wrapper stops advancing and the call never returns. Very deep indentation can also lay out longer than the buffer responsum_aptare allocates (input length x 2 + w x 4 + 64 bytes). Caution (audit 2026-10-18, unfixed): deep indentation is not needed - an ordinary hanging indent (a list or quote marker) can lay out to about 4x the input, and the second disponere pass in responsum_aptare then writes past its piscina allocation (heap overflow).
    symbolum vicus_quaestio_disponere quaestio_textum_planare
  8. quomodohow do I let pictor panes paste images from the clipboard?
    Call vicus_applicatio_imaginis_portam_ponere(&app, porta, ctx) with a function returning an ImagoFructus. All pictor panes share one context and read the port at the moment of the paste, so panes mounted before the call see it too. NIHIL means no image source (the terminal twin has no Cocoa).
    symbolum vicus_applicatio_imaginis_portam_ponere · via apps/vicus/vicus.c
  9. quomodohow do I turn on notifications, the sessions pane and tmux tracking?
    Call vicus_applicatio_admonitiones_aperire(&app, via) once with the path of the praeconium log. Until then $notifications and $sessions are empty, nothing is read and tmux is never asked. Afterwards each pulse follows the log, and every 5 seconds terminal panes attached to tmux sessions are recorded (so a restore re-attaches) and detached ones forgotten. FALSUM if it is already open or the log cannot be followed.
    symbolum vicus_applicatio_admonitiones_aperire

vim

include/vim.h · lib/vim.interrogationes.stml

  1. quomodohow do I drive a vim-style editor over a character sheet?
    Make a TabulaCharacterum, create the state with vim_initiare (or vim_initiare_cum_contextu to attach a clipboard and undo stack), then feed every key through vim_tractare_clavem_cum_tempore and keep the VimStatus it returns. Printable keys are plain characters; arrows, Enter, Tab, Escape and the rest are the negative VIM_CLAVIS_* constants.
    symbolum vim_initiare vim_initiare_cum_contextu vim_tractare_clavem_cum_tempore TabulaCharacterum · via lib/scriba_actiones.c
  2. possumnecan I treat vim_tractare_clavem as a pure function and keep the old status for my own undo?
    No. Only the VimStatus struct is passed by value; the TabulaCharacterum, the VimClipboard and the VimUndoAcervus it points to are edited in place. An old copy of the status still points at the already-changed sheet. Read status.mutatus after a key to learn whether the sheet changed (yank never sets it).
    symbolum vim_tractare_clavem VimStatus
  3. curwhy does typing fd in insert mode leave insert mode?
    fd is the house escape chord: an f followed by d within 0.3 s (FD_TIMEOUT) deletes the f and returns to normal mode. Pass a real clock in seconds to vim_tractare_clavem_cum_tempore. The plain vim_tractare_clavem passes time 0.0 for every key, so with it ANY f followed by d escapes, however slowly they were typed.
    symbolum vim_tractare_clavem_cum_tempore vim_tractare_clavem
  4. quomodohow do I share copy and paste between several editors?
    Allocate one VimClipboard, clear it with vim_clipboard_initiare and pass the same pointer to every editor's vim_initiare_cum_contextu. dd, yy, Y and visual d, x and y fill it; p and P paste below or above. With a NIHIL clipboard those keys still delete but nothing is saved or pasted. Caution (audit 2026-10-18, unfixed): yy then p of a full-width 68-column line pastes only its first 67 columns.
    symbolum vim_clipboard_initiare vim_initiare_cum_contextu VimClipboard
  5. possumnecan the clipboard hold long lines and big selections?
    Only up to its fixed size: VIM_CLIPBOARD_LINEAE_MAXIMAE (XXXII) lines of at most 67 characters (VIM_CLIPBOARD_LINEA_MAXIMA LXVIII minus a NUL), so the last column of a full 68-column line is not copied (the same 67-column cut applies to dd's undo copy). A visual y of more than 32 lines copies only the first 32, and a visual d or x of more than 32 lines DELETES only the first 32 - even with no clipboard attached. Copying is line-wise only: there is no y{motion}.
    symbolum VimClipboard vim_tractare_clavem
  6. possumnecan I undo any edit with u?
    No: only line operations, and only with an undo stack from vim_undo_creare attached. In the code dd, visual d/x, p and P push entries; character edits (x, insert, backspace), o, O, d$, dG, J, >> and << push nothing, although the header also lists o, O and dG. So after an unrecorded edit, u undoes an OLDER line operation instead. Each line is its own entry, so undoing a three-line paste takes three u. The stack is a ring of VIM_UNDO_MAGNITUDO (XXXII); older entries are overwritten. There is no redo. Caution (audit 2026-10-18, unfixed): dd then u on a full-width 68-column line restores only its first 67 columns.
    symbolum vim_undo_creare vim_undo_trudere vim_undo_tollere VimUndoAcervus
  7. quidwhat keys and modes does this vim support?
    Three modes (vim_nomen_modi gives NORMAL, INSERT, VISUAL). Normal: h j k l, 0 $ ^, w b, g G (g alone goes to the top), i a A I o O, V (line-wise visual), x, dd d$ dG, yy Y, J, p P, u, >> <<; Escape in normal mode sets debet_claudere. Visual: j k g G, d x y, >> <<, Escape. Insert: printable ASCII, Backspace, Delete, Enter (splits the line with auto-indent), Tab, arrows, Escape or fd.
    symbolum vim_tractare_clavem vim_nomen_modi ModoVim
  8. quidwhat does G go to, the last line of text or of the sheet?
    The last line of the SHEET (tabula->altitudo - 1), which is usually empty because the sheet has a fixed height. dG, in contrast, deletes from the cursor to the last line that has content. w also stops at the last line with content.
    symbolum vim_tractare_clavem TabulaCharacterum · via lib/vim.worklog.md
  9. quidwhat happens when J joins a line that does not fit on the line above?
    Only whole words that fit move up; the rest stays on the lower line, pulled left to that line's own indentation, so nothing past the margin is lost. If not even the first word fits, nothing changes and mutatus stays FALSUM. Backspace at column 0 in insert mode joins the same way (fixed 2026-10-14; before that the overflow was cut off).
    symbolum vim_tractare_clavem · via lib/vim.worklog.md
  10. quomodohow do I insert a block of text programmatically without auto-indent?
    Set the mode with vim_ponere_modum(status, MODO_VIM_INSERERE), set status.sine_auto_indent = VERUM, send each character as a key and VIM_CLAVIS_ENTER for each newline, then restore the flag and the old mode (pagina_inserere_textum_crudus). Beware the fd escape: with vim_tractare_clavem (time 0.0 for every key, as pagina does) any f followed by d in the text leaves insert mode and the rest of the text runs as normal-mode commands. Use vim_tractare_clavem_cum_tempore with a time that grows by at least 0.3 s (FD_TIMEOUT) per key.
    symbolum vim_ponere_modum vim_tractare_clavem vim_tractare_clavem_cum_tempore VimStatus · via lib/pagina.c

volumen

include/volumen.h · lib/volumen.interrogationes.stml

  1. quidwhat is a volumen and what is inside the file?
    One sqlite file that holds a whole document, so copying the file copies the document. Inside are three tables of its own (plus scrinium's _migrationes bookkeeping): acta, an append-only event log that is the truth; massae, content blobs keyed by SHA-256 hex (identical content is stored once); and plagulae, the manifest from path to sigillum, a projection that can be rebuilt from acta plus massae.
    symbolum volumen_creare volumen_acta_legere volumen_plagulas_enumerare · via lib/volumen.worklog.md
  2. quomodohow do I store a file in a volumen and read it back?
    Open with volumen_aperire_aut_creare(piscina, via), store with volumen_plagulam_condere(vol, via_relativa, contentum, origo) (one transaction: blob, plagula-condita event, manifest upsert), and read with volumen_plagulam_promere(vol, via_relativa, piscina, &inventum). The result is a chorda in YOUR piscina, binary-safe and NOT NUL-terminated; *inventum separates an unknown path (FALSUM) from a file that is legitimately empty (VERUM, mensura 0). Finish with volumen_claudere.
    symbolum volumen_aperire_aut_creare volumen_plagulam_condere volumen_plagulam_promere volumen_claudere
  3. possumnecan I pass an empty origo string to volumen_plagulam_condere?
    No, it returns FALSUM. The origo text is copied with chorda_ex_literis, which gives a NIHIL datum for "" (piscina refuses zero-byte allocations); that binds as SQL NULL and the origo column is NOT NULL. Pass a real label such as "genita" or "vendicata:lib/chorda.c".
    symbolum volumen_plagulam_condere chorda_ex_literis
  4. possumnecan I get a reason when volumen_creare or volumen_aperire returns NIHIL?
    Only on stderr. volumen_creare refuses a path that already exists and volumen_aperire one that does not, each printing one line and returning NIHIL; volumen_error(NIHIL) just says "volumen NIHIL". If volumen_creare fails after sqlite has made the file (the volumen-creatum event not written), the file stays on disk and every retry is refused as already existing. Once a Volumen is open, volumen_error gives the last sqlite message after a FALSUM or 0 return.
    symbolum volumen_creare volumen_aperire volumen_error
  5. quomodohow do I write several files atomically?
    Wrap the calls in volumen_transactionem_incipere and volumen_transactionem_committere (or _revolvere on failure). Inside them volumen_plagulam_condere and volumen_plagulam_removere do not open their own transaction and do not roll back on error: the caller owns it. Nesting is refused: a second incipere returns FALSUM, because sqlite cannot nest transactions.
    symbolum volumen_transactionem_incipere volumen_transactionem_committere volumen_transactionem_revolvere volumen_plagulam_condere
  6. quomodohow do I see what the document looked like at an earlier point?
    volumen_plicam_ad(vol, seq, piscina) rebuilds the manifest by replaying the plagula-condita and plagula-remota events with seq <= seq (seq <= 0 means all, and then equals volumen_plagulas_enumerare, which the test pins); read the old content with volumen_massam_promere on each sigillum_hex. volumen_acta_legere(vol, post_seq, piscina) returns the events AFTER post_seq (exclusive; 0 = all), in seq order. Events whose JSON does not parse are skipped by the fold, not fatal.
    symbolum volumen_plicam_ad volumen_acta_legere volumen_massam_promere
  7. curwhy does removing a file not free its content?
    volumen_plagulam_removere appends a plagula-remota event and deletes the manifest row, but the blob stays in massae: the history still points at it, and volumen_plicam_ad at an earlier seq shows the file again. Removing a path that is not there still returns VERUM (idempotent) and still appends a plagula-remota event. There is no garbage collection of blobs.
    symbolum volumen_plagulam_removere volumen_massam_condere volumen_summa_massarum
  8. quomodohow do I use a volumen in a test without touching the user's real files?
    volumen_temporarium(piscina, praefixum) creates $TMPDIR/praefixum-N.volumen (/tmp when TMPDIR is unset or empty) with the first free N from 1 to C, and volumen_claudere DELETES that file. A test that forgets to close leaves its file behind, and after C leftovers volumen_temporarium returns NIHIL, so close every one. volumen_via tells you which file is really in use.
    symbolum volumen_temporarium volumen_claudere volumen_via
  9. quomodohow do I record my own events in a volumen?
    volumen_actum_appendere(vol, genus, datum) appends to acta and returns the new seq (0 means failure). datum is meant to be JSON but is not checked; an empty genus, or an empty datum whose pointer is NIHIL (what chorda_ex_literis gives for ""), binds as SQL NULL and the NOT NULL columns refuse it; the timestamp (momentum, ISO UTC with milliseconds) is stamped by sqlite, not by C. Store image or other binary payloads with volumen_massam_condere and put the returned hex sigillum in the event's JSON: a blob is content, the event says how it is used.
    symbolum volumen_actum_appendere volumen_acta_legere VolumenActum
  10. possumnecan I keep one Volumen open for the whole life of an application?
    Yes, but every write copies small strings (genus, sigillum hex, origo, the event JSON) into the piscina given at open, and an arena never frees them, so that piscina grows with each write. Read results go into the piscina you pass to each read call instead. The sqlite statements themselves are allocated by sqlite, not the piscina.
    symbolum volumen_plagulam_condere volumen_actum_appendere volumen_aperire

widget

include/widget.h · lib/widget.interrogationes.stml

  1. quomodohow do I put several widgets in one window and route keys and clicks to them?
    Create a ManagerWidget with manager_widget_creare(piscina), register each widget with its datum, a FunctioReddere, a FunctioTractareEventum and a rectangle in CHARACTER cells (manager_widget_registrare), then call manager_widget_tractare_eventum for every event and manager_widget_reddere every frame. Widgets are painted in registration order, each told whether it has focus. schirmata.c and layout.c are the in-tree users. The header's EXEMPLUM shows an older registrare call with no reddere or tractare_eventum arguments; follow the prototype instead.
    symbolum manager_widget_creare manager_widget_registrare manager_widget_tractare_eventum manager_widget_reddere
  2. possumnecan I register more than sixteen widgets with one manager?
    No. The manager holds a fixed array of XVI Widget slots and manager_widget_registrare returns -1 when it is full (or the manager is NIHIL). Check that return value: layout.c ignores it, so a seventeenth widget would silently never draw or receive events. There is no call to unregister or replace a widget: schirmata.c swaps one by writing the datum, reddere and tractare_eventum fields of manager->widgets[i] directly.
    symbolum manager_widget_registrare ManagerWidget
  3. quomodohow does focus move between widgets?
    The first widget registered gets focus. A mouse-down focuses the first widget whose cell rectangle contains the click (manager_widget_focus_ad_punctum), and a Tab key the focused widget did not consume moves focus to the next widget, wrapping (manager_widget_focus_proximum). The manager checks only that the key is CLAVIS_TABULA, never the modifiers, and the macOS fenestra maps the Tab key to CLAVIS_TABULA with or without Shift, so an unconsumed Shift-Tab also moves focus FORWARD: to go back, consume Shift-Tab in your widget and call manager_widget_focus_praecedens. A click outside every widget leaves focus where it was, and the event still goes to the focused widget. Out-of-range indices to ponere_focus are ignored.
    symbolum manager_widget_ponere_focus manager_widget_focus_proximum manager_widget_focus_praecedens manager_widget_focus_ad_punctum manager_widget_tractare_eventum · via lib/fenestra_macos.m
  4. possumnecan I rely on click-to-focus when the font is drawn at scale 2 or more?
    No. manager_widget_reddere passes your scala to the widgets, but manager_widget_tractare_eventum converts the mouse position to cells with a fixed six by eight pixels (scale I), so at any other scale clicks focus the wrong widget. The only in-tree renderer (concha via schirmata_reddere) draws at scale I.
    symbolum manager_widget_tractare_eventum manager_widget_reddere
  5. quomodohow do I detect a double click in a widget?
    The manager does it: a mouse-down within DUPLEX_CLICK_TEMPUS (0.3 s) and DUPLEX_CLICK_DISTANTIA (XII pixels) of the previous one is delivered to the focused widget as EVENTUS_MUS_DUPLEX INSTEAD of EVENTUS_MUS_DEPRESSUS, so the widget does not see a second press. The timer then resets, so a third click starts a new pair. Mouse coordinates reach the widget in window pixels, not relative to the widget.
    symbolum manager_widget_tractare_eventum
  6. quidwhat is a ContextusWidget and does the manager use it?
    A bundle of shared services handed to widget constructors and factories: piscina, intern, and optionally the entity repository, command registry, widget registry and a mode-switch callback. contextus_widget_creare returns NIHIL only when piscina or intern is NIHIL. The ManagerWidget never reads it; it only needs a piscina.
    symbolum contextus_widget_creare ContextusWidget

xar

include/xar.h · lib/xar.interrogationes.stml

  1. quomodohow do I keep pointers into an array valid while it grows?
    Use a Xar: it grows by adding segments (each twice the size of the last) and never reallocates, so a pointer from xar_addere or xar_obtinere stays valid for the life of the piscina. New elements are zeroed unless the array was created with XAR_VEXILLUM_SINE_ZEPHYRUM.
    symbolum xar_addere xar_obtinere
  2. possumnecan I treat a xar as a flat C array (memcpy, qsort, pointer arithmetic)?
    No. The segments are not contiguous: xar_obtinere(x, 0) points into the first segment only. Copy a range out with xar_copiare_ad_tabulam, sort in place with xar_ordinare, and index every element through xar_obtinere.
    symbolum xar_copiare_ad_tabulam xar_ordinare xar_obtinere
  3. quomodohow do I remove an element from the middle of a xar quickly?
    xar_removere_cum_ultimo moves the last element into the hole: O(1), but the order is not kept. At the end, xar_removere_ultimum drops the last element and xar_tollere copies it out first. There is no order-preserving removal from the middle.
    symbolum xar_removere_cum_ultimo xar_removere_ultimum xar_tollere
  4. possumnecan a xar hold more than about 33 million elements?
    Not the ordinary one: its segment table has XXIV entries (about XXXIII M elements with the default first segment of IV), and past that xar_addere prints a refusal to stderr and returns NIHIL rather than truncating silently. Create the array with xar_creare_magnum (LXIV segments) when it really is that large.
    symbolum xar_creare_magnum xar_addere
  5. curwhy must a comparator passed to xar_ordinare not allocate from the array's piscina?
    xar_ordinare is a stable merge sort whose scratch space (2n elements) comes from the array's own piscina between piscina_notare and piscina_reficere. Anything the comparator allocates there is rolled back with the scratch when the sort returns. On a fixed-size piscina with no room left it falls back to an in-place insertion sort (O(n^2), no allocation).
    symbolum xar_ordinare piscina_notare piscina_reficere
